    1→'use client'\n     2→\n     3→import { useRef, useEffect, useCallback, forwardRef, useImperativeHandle, useState } from 'react'\n     4→import { useDesignEditor, type SelectedObjectInfo } from '@/hooks/useDesignEditor'\n     5→import { getCanvasPrintArea } from '@/lib/print-area-config'\n     6→import {\n     7→  drawPrintAreaGuide,\n     8→  drawSafeZone,\n     9→  clampObjectToPrintArea,\n    10→  getCappedDPR,\n    11→  calculateCanvasDimensions,\n    12→  removeGuides,\n    13→  isGuideObject,\n    14→  type PrintAreaRect,\n    15→} from '@/lib/canvas-helpers'\n    16→import { loadFonts, loadFabric } from '@/lib/fabric-init'\n    17→import { colorNameToHex, isLightColor } from '@/lib/color-map'\n    18→import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'\n    19→\n    20→export interface CanvasHandle {\n    21→  addText: (text: string, opts?: { fontFamily?: string; fontSize?: number; fill?: string }) => void\n    22→  addImage: (url: string) => Promise<void>\n    23→  addSVG: (svgText: string) => Promise<void>\n    24→  removeSelected: () => void\n    25→  duplicateSelected: () => void\n    26→  exportPNG: (multiplier?: number) => string\n    27→  exportJSON: () => object\n    28→  loadFromJSON: (json: object) => Promise<void>\n    29→  getSelectedInfo: () => SelectedObjectInfo | null\n    30→  getCanvas: () => any | null\n    31→  // Z-order API\n    32→  bringForward: () => void\n    33→  sendBackward: () => void\n    34→  bringToFront: () => void\n    35→  sendToBack: () => void\n    36→  // Layers API\n    37→  getLayers: () => LayerInfo[]\n    38→  setObjectVisibility: (id: string, visible: boolean) => void\n    39→  setObjectLocked: (id: string, locked: boolean) => void\n    40→}\n    41→\n    42→export interface LayerInfo {\n    43→  id: string\n    44→  type: string\n    45→  name: string\n    46→  visible: boolean\n    47→  locked: boolean\n    48→}\n    49→\n    50→interface CanvasWorkspaceProps {\n    51→  productType: string\n    52→  variantColor: string\n    53→  panelId: string\n    54→  productCategory: string\n    55→  blankImageUrl?: string\n    56→  variantColorHex?: string\n    57→  aspectRatio?: number\n    58→  onHistorySave?: () => void\n    59→  onLayersChange?: (layers: LayerInfo[]) => void\n    60→  className?: string\n    61→}\n    62→\n    63→/** Normalize a variant color name for template file matching */\n    64→function normalizeColorForTemplate(color: string): string {\n    65→  return color.toLowerCase().trim().replace(/\\s+/g, '-')\n    66→}\n    67→\n    68→/** Get blank template URL candidates for a product type and color */\n    69→function getBlankTemplateUrls(productType: string, color: string, panelId: string): string[] {\n    70→  const normalized = normalizeColorForTemplate(color)\n    71→  const panelSuffix = panelId !== 'front' ? `-${panelId}` : ''\n    72→  return [\n    73→    // Try: tshirt-black-front.png (panel-specific)\n    74→    `/mockup-templates/${productType}-${normalized}${panelSuffix}.png`,\n    75→    // Try: tshirt-black.png (generic)\n    76→    `/mockup-templates/${productType}-${normalized}.png`,\n    77→    // Try: tshirt-white.png or tshirt-black.png as closest match\n    78→    `/mockup-templates/${productType}-${isLightColor(colorNameToHex(color)) ? 'white' : 'black'}${panelSuffix}.png`,\n    79→    `/mockup-templates/${productType}-${isLightColor(colorNameToHex(color)) ? 'white' : 'black'}.png`,\n    80→  ]\n    81→}\n    82→\n    83→export const CanvasWorkspace = forwardRef<CanvasHandle, CanvasWorkspaceProps>(\n    84→  function CanvasWorkspace({ productType, variantColor, panelId, productCategory, blankImageUrl, variantColorHex, aspectRatio = 1, onHistorySave, onLayersChange, className }, ref) {\n    85→    const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)\n    86→    const canvasElRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null)\n    87→    const fabricCanvasRef = useRef<any>(null)\n    88→    const printAreaRef = useRef<PrintAreaRect>({ left: 0, top: 0, width: 0, height: 0 })\n    89→    const alignGuidelinesDispose = useRef<(() => void) | null>(null)\n    90→    const [isReady, setIsReady] = useState(false)\n    91→    const { setSelectedObject, setDirty } = useDesignEditor()\n    92→\n    93→    // Build layers list from canvas objects, excluding guides\n    94→    const buildLayers = useCallback((): LayerInfo[] => {\n    95→      const canvas = fabricCanvasRef.current\n    96→      if (!canvas) return []\n    97→      return canvas.getObjects()\n    98→        .filter((obj: any) => !isGuideObject(obj) && obj.data?.type !== 'productBackground')\n    99→        .map((obj: any, i: number) => ({\n   100→          id: obj.data?.id || `layer-${i}`,\n   101→          type: obj.type === 'i-text' || obj.type === 'textbox' ? 'text' : 'image',\n   102→          name: obj.data?.name || (obj.type === 'i-text' || obj.type === 'textbox' ? `Text ${i + 1}` : `Image ${i + 1}`),\n   103→          visible: obj.visible !== false,\n   104→          locked: !obj.selectable,\n   105→        }))\n   106→    }, [])\n   107→\n   108→    const emitLayersChange = useCallback(() => {\n   109→      onLayersChange?.(buildLayers())\n   110→    }, [buildLayers, onLayersChange])\n   111→\n   112→    // Extract selection info from a fabric object\n   113→    const extractObjectInfo = useCallback((obj: any): SelectedObjectInfo | null => {\n   114→      if (!obj || isGuideObject(obj)) return null\n   115→\n   116→      const isText = obj.type === 'i-text' || obj.type === 'text' || obj.type === 'textbox'\n   117→      return {\n   118→        type: isText ? 'text' : 'image',\n   119→        id: obj.data?.id || obj.id || String(Date.now()),\n   120→        text: isText ? obj.text : undefined,\n   121→        fontFamily: isText ? obj.fontFamily : undefined,\n   122→        fontSize: isText ? obj.fontSize : undefined,\n   123→        fill: typeof obj.fill === 'string' ? obj.fill : undefined,\n   124→        textAlign: isText ? obj.textAlign : undefined,\n   125→        fontWeight: isText ? String(obj.fontWeight) : undefined,\n   126→        fontStyle: isText ? obj.fontStyle : undefined,\n   127→        left: obj.left ?? 0,\n   128→        top: obj.top ?? 0,\n   129→        width: obj.getScaledWidth?.() ?? obj.width ?? 0,\n   130→        height: obj.getScaledHeight?.() ?? obj.height ?? 0,\n   131→        angle: obj.angle ?? 0,\n   132→        scaleX: obj.scaleX ?? 1,\n   133→        scaleY: obj.scaleY ?? 1,\n   134→      }\n   135→    }, [])\n   136→\n   137→    // Initialize canvas\n   138→    useEffect(() => {\n   139→      let destroyed = false\n   140→      let canvas: any = null\n   141→\n   142→      async function init() {\n   143→        if (!containerRef.current || !canvasElRef.current) return\n   144→\n   145→        await loadFonts()\n   146→        const fabric = await loadFabric()\n   147→\n   148→        if (destroyed) return\n   149→\n   150→        const container = containerRef.current!\n   151→        const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect()\n   152→        const dims = calculateCanvasDimensions(rect.width, rect.height, aspectRatio)\n   153→        const dpr = getCappedDPR()\n   154→\n   155→        // Fabric.js v6: DPR must be set via config before canvas creation\n   156→        fabric.config.configure({ devicePixelRatio: dpr })\n   157→\n   158→        canvas = new fabric.Canvas(canvasElRef.current!, {\n   159→          width: dims.width,\n   160→          height: dims.height,\n   161→          backgroundColor: '#f5f5f5',\n   162→          selection: true,\n   163→          preserveObjectStacking: true,\n   164→          enableRetinaScaling: true,\n   165→        })\n   166→\n   167→        fabricCanvasRef.current = canvas\n   168→\n   169→        // Load blank garment image as background\n   170→        // Priority: 1) Printful CDN blank image → 2) local template PNGs → 3) hex color solid\n   171→        let templateLoaded = false\n   172→\n   173→        // Helper to add an image as non-selectable background\n   174→        const addBackground = (img: any) => {\n   175→          const scaleX = dims.width / (img.width || dims.width)\n   176→          const scaleY = dims.height / (img.height || dims.height)\n   177→          const scale = Math.max(scaleX, scaleY)\n   178→          img.set({\n   179→            scaleX: scale,\n   180→            scaleY: scale,\n   181→            left: (dims.width - (img.width || 0) * scale) / 2,\n   182→            top: (dims.height - (img.height || 0) * scale) / 2,\n   183→            selectable: false,\n   184→            evented: false,\n   185→            excludeFromExport: true,\n   186→            data: { type: 'productBackground' },\n   187→          })\n   188→          canvas.add(img)\n   189→          canvas.sendObjectToBack(img)\n   190→          templateLoaded = true\n   191→        }\n   192→\n   193→        // Try Printful CDN blank image first (real product photo)\n   194→        if (blankImageUrl && !destroyed) {\n   195→          try {\n   196→            const img = await fabric.FabricImage.fromURL(blankImageUrl, { crossOrigin: 'anonymous' })\n   197→            if (!destroyed) addBackground(img)\n   198→          } catch {\n   199→            // CDN unavailable — fall through to local templates\n   200→          }\n   201→        }\n   202→\n   203→        // Try local template PNGs\n   204→        if (!templateLoaded && !destroyed) {\n   205→          const templateUrls = getBlankTemplateUrls(productType, variantColor, panelId)\n   206→          for (const url of templateUrls) {\n   207→            if (destroyed || templateLoaded) break\n   208→            try {\n   209→              const img = await fabric.FabricImage.fromURL(url, { crossOrigin: 'anonymous' })\n   210→              if (!destroyed) addBackground(img)\n   211→            } catch {\n   212→              // Try next URL\n   213→            }\n   214→          }\n   215→        }\n   216→\n   217→        // Final fallback: hex color as canvas background\n   218→        if (!templateLoaded && !destroyed) {\n   219→          const hex = variantColorHex || colorNameToHex(variantColor)\n   220→          canvas.backgroundColor = hex\n   221→        }\n   222→\n   223→        // Draw print area and safe zone (adapt guide colors to garment color)\n   224→        const printArea = getCanvasPrintArea(productCategory, dims.width, dims.height, panelId)\n   225→        printAreaRef.current = printArea\n   226→        const garmentIsLight = isLightColor(variantColorHex || colorNameToHex(variantColor))\n   227→        await drawPrintAreaGuide(canvas, printArea, garmentIsLight)\n   228→        await drawSafeZone(canvas, printArea, garmentIsLight)\n   229→\n   230→        // Initialize alignment guidelines (built-in Fabric.js v6 extension)\n   231→        try {\n   232→          const { initAligningGuidelines } = await import('fabric/extensions')\n   233→          alignGuidelinesDispose.current = initAligningGuidelines(canvas, {\n   234→            margin: 4,\n   235→            width: 1,\n   236→            color: 'rgba(255, 0, 0, 0.75)',\n   237→          })\n   238→        } catch {\n   239→          // Extension not available — skip snap guides\n   240→        }\n   241→\n   242→        // Event listeners\n   243→        canvas.on('selection:created', (e: any) => {\n   244→          const obj = e.selected?.[0]\n   245→          if (obj) setSelectedObject(extractObjectInfo(obj))\n   246→        })\n   247→\n   248→        canvas.on('selection:updated', (e: any) => {\n   249→          const obj = e.selected?.[0]\n   250→          if (obj) setSelectedObject(extractObjectInfo(obj))\n   251→        })\n   252→\n   253→        canvas.on('selection:cleared', () => {\n   254→          setSelectedObject(null)\n   255→        })\n   256→\n   257→        canvas.on('object:modified', (e: any) => {\n   258→          setDirty(true)\n   259→          onHistorySave?.()\n   260→          const obj = e.target\n   261→          if (obj) setSelectedObject(extractObjectInfo(obj))\n   262→          emitLayersChange()\n   263→        })\n   264→\n   265→        canvas.on('object:moving', (e: any) => {\n   266→          const obj = e.target\n   267→          if (obj && !isGuideObject(obj)) {\n   268→            clampObjectToPrintArea(obj, printAreaRef.current)\n   269→          }\n   270→        })\n   271→\n   272→        canvas.on('text:changed', () => {\n   273→          setDirty(true)\n   274→          onHistorySave?.()\n   275→          const active = canvas.getActiveObject()\n   276→          if (active) setSelectedObject(extractObjectInfo(active))\n   277→        })\n   278→\n   279→        canvas.on('object:added', () => emitLayersChange())\n   280→        canvas.on('object:removed', () => emitLayersChange())\n   281→\n   282→        // Restore saved panel state from Zustand (if switching panels)\n   283→        const savedPanelState = useDesignEditor.getState().panelStates[panelId]\n   284→        if (savedPanelState?.fabricJson) {\n   285→          try {\n   286→            await canvas.loadFromJSON(savedPanelState.fabricJson)\n   287→            canvas.renderAll()\n   288→          } catch {\n   289→            // Failed to restore — start fresh\n   290→   50→    <>\n    51→      <header className=\"flex items-center justify-between h-14 px-4 border-b border-border bg-card shrink-0\">\n    52→        {/* Left: Back + Product name */}\n    53→        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0\">\n    54→          <Button variant=\"ghost\" size=\"icon\" onClick={handleBack} className=\"shrink-0\">\n    55→            <ArrowLeft '@/hooks/useDesignEditor'\n     8→import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'\n     9→\n    10→interface LayersPanelProps {\n    11→  onBringForward: () => void\n    12→  onSendBackward: () => void\n    13→  onBringToFront: () => void\n    14→  onSendToBack: () => void\n    15→  onToggleVisibility: (id: string, visible: boolean) => void\n    16→  onToggleLock: (id: string, locked: boolean) => void\n    17→}\n    18→\n    19→export function LayersPanel({\n    20→  onBringForward,\n    21→  onSendBackward,\n    22→  onBringToFront,\n    23→  onSendToBack,\n    24→  onToggleVisibility,\n    25→  onToggleLock,\n    26→}: LayersPanelProps) {\n    27→  const t = useTranslations('designEditor.layers')\n    28→  const { layers, selectedObject } = useDesignEditor()\n    29→\n    30→  if }\n    37→\n    38→  // Reverse to show top layers first\n    39→  const reversedLayers = [...layers].reverse()\n    40→\n    41→  return (\n    42→    <div className=\"space-y-2 p-3\">\n    43→      <h3 className=\"text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground\">\n    44→        {t('title')}\n    45→      </h3>\n    46→\n    47→      {/* Z-order controls for selected object */}\n    48→      {selectedObject && (\n    49→        <div className=\"flex gap-1\">\n    50→          <Button variant=\"outline\" size=\"icon\" className=\"size-7\" onClick={onBringToFront} title={t('bringToFront')}>\n    51→            <ChevronsUp className=\"size-3.5\" />\n    52→          </Button>\n    53→          <Button variant=\"outline\" size=\"icon\" className=\"size-7\" onClick={onBringForward} title={t('bringForward')}>\n    54→            <ChevronUp className=\"size-3.5\" />\n    55→          </Button>\n    56→          <Button variant=\"outline\" size=\"icon\" className=\"size-7\" onClick={onSendBackward} title={t('sendBackward')}>\n    57→            <ChevronDown className=\"size-3.5\" />\n    58→          </Button>\n    59→          <Button variant=\"outline\" size=\"icon\" className=\"size-7\" onClick={onSendToBack} title={t('sendToBack')}>\n    60→            <ChevronsDown className=\"size-3.5\" />\n    61→          </Button>\n    62→        </div>\n    63→      )}\n    64→\n    65→      <Separator />\n    66→\n    67→      {/* Layer list */}\n    68→      <div className=\"space-y-0.5\">\n    69→        {reversedLayers.map((layer) => {\n    70→          const isSelected = selectedObject?.id === layer.id\n    71→          const TypeIcon = layer.type === 'text' ? Type : ImageIcon\n    72→\n    73→          return (\n    74→            <div\n    75→              key={layer.id}\n    76→              className={cn(\n    77→                'flex items-center gap-2 px-2 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs',\n    78→                isSelected ? (blur: number) => void\n    49→  onShadowOffsetXChange?: (x: number) => void\n    50→  onShadowOffsetYChange?: (y: number) => void\n    51→  onOutlineToggle?: (enabled: boolean) => void\n    52→ selectedBold = false,\n    68→  selectedItalic = false,\n    69→  shadowEnabled = false,\n    70→  shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)',\n    71→  shadowBlur = 10,\n    72→  shadowOffsetX = 5,\n    73→  shadowOffsetY = 5,\n    74→  outlineEnabled = false,\n    75→  outlineColor = '#000000',\n    76→  outlineWidth = 2,\n    77→  fillMode = 'solid',\n    78→  gradientStartColor = '#ff0000',\n    79→  gradientEndColor = '#0000ff',\n    80→  gradientAngle = 0,\n    81→  onFontChange,\n    82→  onFontSizeChange,\n    83→  onColorChange,\n    84→  onAlignChange,\n    85→  onBoldToggle,\n    86→  onItalicToggle,\n    87→  onShadowToggle,\n    88→  onShadowColorChange,\n    89→  onShadowBlurChange,\n    90→  onShadowOffsetXChange,\n    91→  onShadowOffsetYChange,\n    92→  onOutlineToggle,\n    93→  onOutlineColorChange,\n    94→  onOutlineWidthChange,\n    95→  onFillModeChange,\n    96→  onGradientStartColorChange,\n    97→  onGradientEndColorChange,\n    98→  onGradientAngleChange,\n    99→}: TextToolProps) {\n   100→  const t = useTranslations('designEditor')\n   101→\n   102→  return (\n   103→    <div className=\"space-y-4 p-3\">\n   104→      {/* Add new text */}\n   105→      <div className=\"space-y-1.5\">\n   106→        <Button\n   107→          variant=\"outline\"\n   108→          size=\"sm\"\n   109→          className=\"w-full gap-2\"\n   110→          onClick={() => onAddText(t('text.placeholder'))}\n   111→        >\n   112→          <Type className=\"size-4\" />\n   113→          {t('text.addText')}\n   114→        </Button>\n   115→      </div>\n   116→\n   117→      {/* Properties for selected text */}\n   118→      {selectedText !== undefined && (\n   119→        <>\n   120→          <FontPicker\n   121→            value={selectedFont}\n   122→            onChange={(f) => onFontChange?.(f)}\n   123→          />\n   124→\n   125→          <div className=\"space-y-1.5\">\n   126→            <Label className=\"text-xs\">{t('properties.fontSize')}</Label>\n   127→            <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n   128→              <Slider\n   129→                value={[selectedFontSize]}\n   130→                onValueChange={([v]) => onFontSizeChange?.(v)}\n   131→                min={12}\n   132→                max={120}\n   133→                step={1}\n   134→                className=\"flex-1\"\n   135→              />\n   136→              <span className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground w-8 text-right\">{selectedFontSize}</span>\n   137→            </div>\n   138→          </div>\n   139→\n   140→          {/* Fill: Solid / Gradient */}\n   141→          <GradientEditor\n   142→            mode={fillMode}\n   143→            solidColor={selectedColor}\n   144→            startColor={gradientStartColor}\n   145→            endColor={gradientEndColor}\n   146→            angle={gradientAngle}\n   147→            onModeChange={(m) => onFillModeChange?.(m)}\n   148→            onSolidColorChange={(c) => onColorChange?.(c)}\n   149→            onStartColorChange={(c) => onGradientStartColorChange?.(c)}\n   150→            onEndColorChange={(c) => onGradientEndColorChange?.(c)}\n   151→            onAngleChange={(a) => onGradientAngleChange?.(a)}\n   152→          />\n   153→\n   154→          {/* Alignment */}\n   155→          <div className=\"space-y-1.5\">\n   156→            <Label className=\"text-xs\">{t('properties.alignment')}</Label>\n   157→            <div className=\"flex gap-1\">\n   158→              {[\n   159→                { value: 'left', icon: AlignLeft },\n   160→                { value: 'center', icon: AlignCenter },\n   161→                { value: 'right', icon: AlignRight },\n   162→              ].map(({ value, icon: Icon }) => (\n   163→                <Button\n   164→                  key={value}\n   165→                  variant={selectedAlign === value ? 'default' : 'outline'}\n   166→                  size=\"icon\"\n   167→                  className=\"size-8\"\n   168→                  onClick={() => onAlignChange?.(value)}\n   169→                >\n   170→                  <Icon className=\"size-4\" />\n   171→                </Button>\n   172→              ))}\n   173→            </div>\n   174→          </div>\n   175→\n   176→          {/* Bold / Italic */}\n   177→          <div className=\"flex gap-1\">\n   178→            <Button\n   179→              variant={selectedBold ? 'default' : 'outline'}\n   180→              size=\"icon\"\n   181→              className=\"size-8\"\n   182→              onClick={onBoldToggle}\n   183→            >\n   184→              <Bold className=\"size-4\" />\n   185→            </Button>\n   186→            <Button\n   187→              variant={selectedItalic ?   <Switch checked={outlineEnabled} onCheckedChange={(v) => onOutlineToggle?.(v)} />\n   203→            </div>\n   204→            {outlineEnabled && (\n   205→              <div className=\"space-y-2 pl-1\">\n   206→                <ColorPicker value={outlineColor} onChange={(c) => onOutlineColorChange?.(c)} />\n   207→                <div className=\"space-y-1\">\n   208→                  <div {"parentUuid":"9d73b239-4f7a-4377-abd8-b90c05f4fbd2","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend","sessionId":"395964ae-6f7f-4fea-bbec-d5524241999b","version":"2.1.66","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a613487f37963273d","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013X5eytTrveYeQ6SQ4NV7ci","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→'use client'\n     2→\n     3→import { useRef, useCallback, useState } from 'react'\n     4→import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'\n     5→import { Upload } from 'lucide-react'\n     6→import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'\n     7→import { toast } from 'sonner'\n     8→\n     9→const MAX_FILE_SIZE = 10 * 1024 * 1024 // 10MB\n    10→const ACCEPTED_TYPES = ['image/png', 'image/jpeg', 'image/svg+xml', 'image/webp']\n    11→\n    12→interface ImageToolProps {\n    13→  onImageAdd: (url: string) => Promise<void>\n    14→  onSVGAdd?: (svgText: string) => Promise<void>\n    15→}\n    16→\n    17→export function ImageTool({ onImageAdd, onSVGAdd }: ImageToolProps) {\n    18→  const t = useTranslations('designEditor')\n    19→  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)\n    20→  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false)\n    21→  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false)\n    22→\n    23→  const processFile = useCallback(async (file: File) => {\n    24→    if (!ACCEPTED_TYPES.includes(file.type)) {\n    25→      toast.error('Unsupported file format')\n    26→      return\n    27→    }\n    28→    if (file.size > MAX_FILE_SIZE) {\n    29→      toast.error('File too large (max 10MB)')\n    30→      return\n    31→    }\n    32→\n    33→    setIsLoading(true)\n    34→    try {\n    35→      // SVG: import as vector (not rasterized)\n    36→      if (file.type === 'image/svg+xml' && onSVGAdd) {\n    37→        const svgText = await file.text()\n    38→        await onSVGAdd(svgText)\n    39→      } else {\n    40→        const url = URL.createObjectURL(file)\n    41→        await onImageAdd(url)\n    42→      }\n    43→    } catch {\n    44→      toast.error('Failed to add image')\n    45→    } finally {\n    46→      setIsLoading(false)\n    47→    }\n    48→  }, [onImageAdd, onSVGAdd])\n    49→\n    50→  const handleFileChange = useCallback((e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {\n    51→    const file = e.target.files?.[0]\n    52→    if (file) processFile(file)\n    53→    // Reset input so same file can be selected again\n    54→    e.target.value = ''\n    55→  }, [processFile])\n    56→\n    57→  const handleDrop = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {\n    58→    e.preventDefault()\n    59→    setIsDragging(false)\n    60→    const file = e.dataTransfer.files[0]\n    61→    if (file) processFile(file)\n    62→  }, [processFile])\n    63→\n    64→  return (\n    65→    <div className=\"space-y-4 p-3\">\n    66→      <input\n    67→        ref={inputRef}\n    68→        type=\"file\"\n    69→        accept={ACCEPTED_TYPES.join(',')}\n    70→        onChange={handleFileChange}\n    71→        className=\"hidden\"\n    72→      />\n    73→\n    74→      <div\n    75→        onDragOver={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setIsDragging(true) }}\n    76→        onDragLeave={() => setIsDragging(false)}\n    77→        onDrop={handleDrop}\n    78→        onClick={() => from '@/components/ui/input'\n     7→import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'\n     8→\n     9→const PRESET_COLORS = [\n    10→  '#000000', '#FFFFFF', '#FF0000', '#00FF00',\n    11→  '#0000FF', '#FFFF00', '#FF00FF', '#00FFFF',\n    12→  '#FF6B35', '#7C3AED', '#059669', '#DC2626',\n    13→  '#2563EB', '#D97706', '#DB2777', '#6366F1',\n    14→]\n    15→\n    16→interface ColorPickerProps {\n    17→  value: string\n    18→  onChange: (color: string) => void\n    19→}\n    20→\n    21→export function ColorPicker({ value, onChange }: ColorPickerProps) {\n    22→  const t =    if (/^#[0-9A-Fa-f]{6}$/.test(hex)) {\n    28→      onChange(hex)\n    29→    }\n    30→  }\n    31→\n    32→  return (\n    33→    <div className=\"space-y-1.5\">\n    34→      <Label       className={cn(\n    45→              'size-7 rounded-md border-2 transition-all hover:scale-110',\n    46→              value === color ? 'border-primary ring-2 ring-ring' : 'border-border'\n    47→            )}\n    48→            style={{ backgroundColor: color }}\n    49→            aria-label={color}\n    50→          />\n    51→        ))}\n    52→      </div>\n    53→      <Input\n    54→        value={hexInput}\n    55→        onChange={(e) => handleHexChange(e.target.value)}\n    56→        placeholder=\"#000000\"\n    57→        className=\"h-8 text-xs font-mono\"\n    58→      />\n    59→    </div>\n    60→  )\n    61→}\n    62→"}]},"uuid":"07a286ab-5373-4dd7-82bf-a9035c497591","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:41:18.626Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d8888141-095e-4c66-8795-20b4584932c2"}

    1→'use client'\n     2→\n     3→import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'\n     4→import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label'\n     5→import { Slider } from '@/components/ui/slider'\n     6→import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'\n     7→import { ColorPicker } from './ColorPicker'\n     8→\n     9→export type FillMode = 'solid' | 'linear' | 'radial'\n    10→\n    11→interface GradientEditorProps {\n    12→  mode: FillMode\n    13→  solidColor: string\n    14→  startColor: string\n    15→  endColor: string\n    16→  angle: number\n    17→  onModeChange: (mode: FillMode) => void\n    18→  onSolidColorChange: (color: string) => void\n    19→  onStartColorChange: (color: string) => void\n    20→  onEndColorChange: (color: string) => void\n    21→  onAngleChange: (angle: number) => void\n    22→}\n    23→\n    24→export function GradientEditor({\n    25→  mode,\n    26→  solidColor,\n    27→  startColor,\n    28→  endColor,\n    29→  angle,\n    30→  onModeChange,\n    31→  onSolidColorChange,\n    32→  onStartColorChange,\n    33→  onEndColorChange,\n    34→  onAngleChange,\n    35→}: GradientEditorProps) {\n    36→  const t = useTranslations('designEditor.effects')\n    37→\n    38→  const modes: FillMode[] = ['solid', 'linear', 'radial']\n    39→\n    40→  return (\n    41→    <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n    42→      <Label className=\"text-xs\">{t('gradient')}</Label>\n    43→\n    44→      {/* Mode selector */}\n    45→      <div className=\"flex gap-1\">\n    46→        {modes.map((m) => (\n    47→          <Button\n    48→            key={m}\n    49→            variant={mode === m ? 'default' : 'outline'}\n    50→            size=\"sm\"\n    51→            className=\"flex-1 text-xs h-7\"\n    52→            onClick={() => onModeChange(m)}\n    53→          >\n    54→            {t(m)}\n    55→          </Button>\n    56→        ))}\n    57→      </div>\n    58→\n    59→      {mode === 'solid' && (\n    60→        <ColorPicker value={solidColor} onChange={onSolidColorChange} />\n    61→      )}\n    62→\n    63→      {(mode === 'linear' || mode === 'radial') && (\n    64→        <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n    65→          <div className=\"space-y-1\">\n    66→            <span className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">{t('startColor')}</span>\n    67→            <ColorPicker value={startColor} onChange={onStartColorChange} />\n    68→          </div>\n    69→          <div className=\"space-y-1\">\n    70→            <span className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">{t('endColor')}</span>\n    71→            <ColorPicker value={endColor} onChange={onEndColorChange} />\n    72→          </div>\n    73→          {mode === 'linear' && (\n    74→            <div className=\"space-y-1\">\n    75→              <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n    76→                <span className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">{t('angle')}</span>\n    77→                <span className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground w-8 text-right\">{angle}°</span>\n    78→              </div>\n    79→              <Slider value={[angle]} onValueChange={([v]) => onAngleChange(v)} min={0} max={360} step={1} />\n    80→            </div>\n    81→          )}\n    82→        </div>\n    83→      )}\n    84→    </div>\n    85→  )\n    86→}\n    87→"}]},"uuid":"9a0bc7fd-1404-4b09-af28-476572cd837e","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:41:18.777Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3da6e2c6-94bd-4aa6-b8a1-a97510b4df54"}

{"parentUuid":"de9c0d9e-9873-42ff-a83d-3dc258903557","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend","sessionId":"395964ae-6f7f-4fea-bbec-d5524241999b","version":"2.1.66","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a613487f37963273d","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XTarTFd7TwbaZEaXWpUnmA","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→/**\n     2→ * Canvas history hook for undo/redo functionality.\n     3→ * Stores JSON snapshots of canvas state, debounced to avoid capturing every pixel of a drag.\n     4→ * Guide objects (printAreaGuide, safeZone, productBackground) are excluded from snapshots.\n     5→ */\n     6→\n     7→import { useCallback, useRef } from 'react'\n     8→import type { Canvas, FabricObject } from 'fabric'\n     9→\n    10→const MAX_STATES = 20\n    11→const DEBOUNCE_MS = 300\n    12→\n    13→/** Types of guide objects that should be excluded from history snapshots */\n    14→const GUIDE_TYPES = new Set(['printAreaGuide', 'safeZone', 'productBackground'])\n    15→\n    16→interface CanvasHistoryReturn {\n    17→  saveState: () => void\n    18→  undo: () => void\n    19→  redo: () => void\n    20→  canUndo: boolean\n    21→  canRedo: boolean\n    22→  clear: () => void\n    23→}\n    24→\n    25→type FabricObjectWithData = FabricObject & { data?: { type?: string } }\n    26→\n    27→/** Filter out guide objects from a serialized canvas JSON */\n    28→function filterGuideObjects(canvasJson: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, unknown> {\n    29→  const filtered = { ...canvasJson }\n    30→  if (Array.isArray(filtered.objects)) {\n    31→    filtered.objects = (filtered.objects as Record<string, unknown>[]).filter((obj) => {\n    32→      const data = obj.data as { type?: string } | undefined\n    33→      return !data?.type || !GUIDE_TYPES.has(data.type)\n    34→    })\n    35→  }\n    36→  return filtered\n    37→}\n    38→\n    39→export function useCanvasHistory(\n    40→  canvasRef: React.RefObject<Canvas | null>,\n    41→  onHistoryChange?: (canUndo: boolean, canRedo: boolean) => void,\n    42→  onAfterRestore?: () => void\n    43→): CanvasHistoryReturn {\n    44→  const states = useRef<string[]>([])\n    45→  const currentIndex = useRef(-1)\n    46→  const isRestoring = useRef(false)\n    47→  const debounceTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null)\n    48→\n    49→  const getCanUndo = () => currentIndex.current > 0\n    50→  const getCanRedo = () => currentIndex.current < states.current.length - 1\n    51→\n    52→  const notifyChange = useCallback(() => {\n    53→    onHistoryChange?.(getCanUndo(), getCanRedo())\n    54→  }, [onHistoryChange])\n    55→\n    56→  const saveState = useCallback(() => {\n    57→    if (isRestoring.current) return\n    58→    const canvas = canvasRef.current\n    59→    if (!canvas) return\n    60→\n    61→    // Debounce rapid changes (e.g., during drag)\n    62→    if (debounceTimer.current) {\n    63→      clearTimeout(debounceTimer.current)\n    64→    }\n    65→\n    66→    debounceTimer.current = setTimeout(() => {\n    67→      const rawJson = canvas.toObject(['data'])\n    68→      // Filter out guide objects so they don't duplicate on undo/redo\n    69→      const filtered = filterGuideObjects(rawJson)\n    70→      const json = JSON.stringify(filtered)\n    71→\n    72→      // Truncate redo history when new action is taken\n    73→      if (currentIndex.current < states.current.length - 1) {\n    74→        states.current = states.current.slice(0, currentIndex.current + 1)\n    75→      }\n    76→\n    77→      states.current.push(json)\n    78→\n    79→      // Cap at MAX_STATES\n    80→      if (states.current.length > MAX_STATES) {\n    81→        states.current.shift()\n    82→      } else {\n    83→        currentIndex.current++\n    84→      }\n    85→\n    86→      notifyChange()\n    87→    }, DEBOUNCE_MS)\n    88→  }, [canvasRef, notifyChange])\n    89→\n    90→  const undo = useCallback(() => {\n    91→    const canvas = canvasRef.current\n    92→    if (!canvas || !getCanUndo()) return\n    93→\n    94→    isRestoring.current = true\n    95→    currentIndex.current--\n    96→    const json = states.current[currentIndex.current]\n    97→\n    98→    // Remove user objects (keep guides), then load snapshot, then redraw guides\n    99→    const userObjects = canvas.getObjects().filter(\n   100→      (obj) => {\n   101→        const o = obj as FabricObjectWithData\n   102→        return !o.data?.type || !GUIDE_TYPES.has(o.data.type)\n   103→      }\n   104→    )\n   105→    userObjects.forEach((obj) => canvas.remove(obj))\n   106→\n   107→    canvas.loadFromJSON(JSON.parse(json)).then(() => {\n   108→      canvas.renderAll()\n   109→      isRestoring.current = false\n   110→      onAfterRestore?.()\n   111→      notifyChange()\n   112→    })\n   113→  }, [canvasRef, notifyChange, onAfterRestore])\n   114→\n   115→  const redo = useCallback(() => {\n   116→    const canvas = canvasRef.current\n   117→    if (!canvas || !getCanRedo()) return\n   118→\n   119→    isRestoring.current = true\n   120→    currentIndex.current++\n   121→    const json = states.current[currentIndex.current]\n   122→\n   123→    // Remove user objects, keep guides\n   124→    const userObjects = canvas.getObjects().filter(\n   125→      (obj) => {\n   126→        const o = obj as FabricObjectWithData\n   127→        return !o.data?.type || !GUIDE_TYPES.has(o.data.type)\n   128→      }\n   129→    )\n   130→    userObjects.forEach((obj) => canvas.remove(obj))\n   131→\n   132→    canvas.loadFromJSON(JSON.parse(json)).then(() => {\n   133→      canvas.renderAll()\n   134→      isRestoring.current = false\n   135→      onAfterRestore?.()\n   136→      notifyChange()\n   137→    })\n   138→  }, [canvasRef, notifyChange, onAfterRestore])\n   139→\n   140→  const clear = useCallback(() => {\n   141→    states.current = []\n   142→    currentIndex.current = -1\n   143→    if (debounceTimer.current) {\n   144→      clearTimeout(debounceTimer.current)\n   145→    }\n   146→    notifyChange()\n   147→  }, [notifyChange])\n   148→\n   149→  return {\n   150→    saveState,\n   151→    undo,\n   152→    redo,\n   153→    canUndo: getCanUndo(),\n   154→    canRedo: getCanRedo(),\n   155→    clear,\n   156→  }\n   157→}\n     1→/**\n     2→ * Hook for saving and loading design compositions to/from Supabase.\n     3→ */\n     4→\n     5→import { useCallback, useState } from 'react'\n     6→\n     7→interface SaveCompositionParams {\n     8→  fabricJson: object\n     9→  previewDataUrl: string\n    10→  productType: string\n    11→  productId: string\n    12→  compositionId?: string\n    13→}\n    14→\n    15→interface SaveResult {\n    16→  composition_id: string\n    17→  preview_url: string\n    18→}\n    19→\n    20→interface LoadResult {\n    21→  id: string\n    22→  schema_version: number\n    23→  layers: object\n    24→  product_type: string\n    25→  product_id: string\n    26→  preview_url: string\n    27→  status: string\n    28→}\n    29→\n    30→export function useDesignPersistence() {\n    31→  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false)\n    32→  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false)\n    33→\n    34→  const save = useCallback(async (params: SaveCompositionParams): Promise<SaveResult | null> => {\n    35→    setIsSaving(true)\n    36→    try {\n    37→      const res = await fetch('/api/designs/compose-v2', {\n    38→        method: 'POST',\n    39→        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n    40→        body: JSON.stringify(params),\n    41→      })\n    42→\n    43→      if (!res.ok) {\n    44→        const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({ error: 'Save failed' }))\n    45→        throw new Error(err.error || 'Save failed')\n    46→      }\n    47→\n    48→      return await res.json()\n    49→    } catch (error) {\n    50→      console.error('Failed to save composition:', error)\n    51→      return null\n    52→    } finally {\n    53→      setIsSaving(false)\n    54→    }\n    55→  }, [])\n    56→\n    57→  const load = useCallback(async (compositionId: string): Promise<LoadResult | null> => {\n    58→    setIsLoading(true)\n    59→    try {\n    60→      const res = await fetch(`/api/designs/composition/${compositionId}`)\n    61→      if (!res.ok) return null\n    62→      return await res.json()\n    63→    } catch {\n    64→      return null\n    65→    } finally {\n    66→      setIsLoading(false)\n    67→    }\n    68→  }, [])\n    69→\n    70→  return { save, load, isSaving, isLoading }\n    71→}\n    72→"}]},"uuid":"1546b29d-006c-456c-b869-9f09332360ed","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:41:21.308Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"cef44eca-0390-4bbf-a594-f1d011706ee2"}

{"parentUuid":"3fa3b54c-1370-4af2-9cee-af711cbfdb69","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend","sessionId":"395964ae-6f7f-4fea-bbec-d5524241999b","version":"2.1.66","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a613487f37963273d","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WxKaXLPAj61JkxFjV7hfXy","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→/**\n     2→ * Canvas helper utilities for Design Studio.\n     3→ * Handles print area guides, object boundary clamping, and DPR management.\n     4→ */\n     5→\n     6→import type { Canvas, FabricObject } from 'fabric'\n     7→\n     8→export interface PrintAreaRect {\n     9→  left: number\n    10→  top: number\n    11→  width: number\n    12→  height: number\n    13→}\n    14→\n    15→/** FabricObject with custom data property (used for guides, user objects, etc.) */\n    16→type FabricObjectWithData = FabricObject & { data?: { type?: string; id?: string } }\n    17→\n    18→/**\n    19→ * Draw the print area boundary guide on the canvas.\n    20→ * Creates a dashed rectangle that is not selectable or exportable.\n    21→ */\n    22→export async function drawPrintAreaGuide(\n    23→  canvas: Canvas,\n    24→  area: PrintAreaRect,\n    25→  isLightBackground: boolean = false\n    26→): Promise<FabricObject> {\n    27→  const { Rect } = await import('fabric')\n    28→  const guide = new Rect({\n    29→    left: area.left,\n    30→    top: area.top,\n    31→    width: area.width,\n    32→    height: area.height,\n    33→    fill: 'transparent',\n    34→    stroke: isLightBackground ? 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35)' : 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.6)',\n    35→    strokeWidth: 1.5,\n    36→    strokeDashArray: [8, 4],\n    37→    selectable: false,\n    38→    evented: false,\n    39→    excludeFromExport: true,\n    40→    data: { type: 'printAreaGuide' },\n    41→  })\n    42→  canvas.add(guide)\n    43→  return guide\n    44→}\n    45→\n    46→/**\n    47→ * Draw the safe zone (10% inset from print area).\n    48→ * Content should ideally stay within this zone.\n    49→ */\n    50→export async function drawSafeZone(\n    51→  canvas: Canvas,\n    52→  area: PrintAreaRect,\n    53→  isLightBackground: boolean = false\n    54→): Promise<FabricObject> {\n    55→  const { Rect } = await import('fabric')\n    56→  const inset = 0.1\n    57→  const safeZone = new Rect({\n    58→    left: area.left + area.width * inset,\n    59→    top: area.top + area.height * inset,\n    60→    width: area.width * (1 - 2 * inset),\n    61→    height: area.height * (1 - 2 * inset),\n    62→    fill: 'transparent',\n    63→    stroke: isLightBackground ? 'rgba(22, 163, 74, 0.5)' : 'rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.4)',\n    64→    strokeWidth: 1,\n    65→    strokeDashArray: [4, 4],\n    66→    selectable: false,\n    67→    evented: false,\n    68→    excludeFromExport: true,\n    69→    data: { type: 'safeZone' },\n    70→  })\n    71→  canvas.add(safeZone)\n    72→  return safeZone\n    73→}\n    74→\n    75→/**\n    76→ * Clamp an object within the print area boundaries.\n    77→ * Called on object:moving to prevent designs from going outside the printable area.\n    78→ */\n    79→export function clampObjectToPrintArea(\n    80→  obj: FabricObject,\n    81→  area: PrintAreaRect\n    82→): void {\n    83→  const bound = obj.getBoundingRect()\n    84→  let newLeft = obj.left ?? 0\n    85→  let newTop = obj.top ?? 0\n    86→\n    87→  if (bound.left < area.left) {\n    88→    newLeft = area.left + (newLeft - bound.left)\n    89→  }\n    90→  if (bound.top < area.top) {\n    91→    newTop = area.top + (newTop - bound.top)\n    92→  }\n    93→  if (bound.left + bound.width > area.left + area.width) {\n    94→    newLeft = area.left + area.width - bound.width + (newLeft - bound.left)\n    95→  }\n    96→  if (bound.top + bound.height > area.top + area.height) {\n    97→    newTop = area.top + area.height - bound.height + (newTop - bound.top)\n    98→  }\n    99→\n   100→  obj.set({ left: newLeft, top: newTop })\n   101→  obj.setCoords()\n   102→}\n   103→\n   104→/**\n   105→ * Get capped DPR for canvas rendering.\n   106→ * iOS Safari has a 256MB canvas memory limit — cap at 2x to prevent crashes.\n   107→ */\n   108→export function getCappedDPR(): number {\n   109→  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return 1\n   110→  return Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2)\n   111→}\n   112→\n   113→/**\n   114→ * Calculate canvas dimensions to fit within a container while maintaining aspect ratio.\n   115→ */\n   116→export function calculateCanvasDimensions(\n   117→  containerWidth: number,\n   118→  containerHeight: number,\n   119→  aspectRatio: number = 1\n   120→): { width: number; height: number } {\n   121→  let width = containerWidth\n   122→  let height = containerWidth / aspectRatio\n   123→\n   124→  if (height > containerHeight) {\n   125→    height = containerHeight\n   126→    width = containerHeight * aspectRatio\n   127→  }\n   128→\n   129→  return {\n   130→    width: Math.floor(width),\n   131→    height: Math.floor(height),\n   132→  }\n   133→}\n   134→\n   135→/**\n   136→ * Remove all guide objects (print area + safe zone) from canvas.\n   137→ */\n   138→export function removeGuides(canvas: Canvas): void {\n   139→  const guides = canvas.getObjects().filter(\n   140→    obj => (obj as FabricObjectWithData).data?.type === 'printAreaGuide' || (obj as FabricObjectWithData).data?.type === 'safeZone'\n   141→  )\n   142→  guides.forEach(g => canvas.remove(g))\n   143→}\n   144→\n   145→/**\n   146→ * Check if an object is a guide (non-exportable helper).\n   147→ */\n   148→export function isGuideObject(obj: FabricObject): boolean {\n   149→  const o = obj as FabricObjectWithData\n   150→  return o.data?.type === 'printAreaGuide' || o.data?.type === 'safeZone'\n   151→}\n   152→"}]},"uuid":"6c279766-09e0-446c-b6e2-e84ef26de5c9","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:41:21.564Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3fa3b54c-1370-4af2-9cee-af711cbfdb69"}

{"parentUuid":"4d26c5c1-13bb-45ff-9ad0-c5918e94022d","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend","sessionId":"395964ae-6f7f-4fea-bbec-d5524241999b","version":"2.1.66","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a613487f37963273d","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01A8k7c7gzSzfTe5FmPYW2RG","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→/**\n     2→ * Print area configuration for the Design Studio canvas.\n     3→ *\n     4→ * The canvas aspect ratio now matches the real product print area (derived from\n     5→ * PRODUCTION_DIMENSIONS). The print area guide is simply the canvas with padding,\n     6→ * since the canvas itself represents the printable surface.\n     7→ */\n     8→\n     9→import { PRINT_AREAS, MULTI_PANEL_AREAS, CATEGORY_TO_PRODUCT_TYPE, type PrintArea } from '@/lib/print-areas'\n    10→\n    11→/** Padding fraction around the print area within the canvas (10% each side) */\n    12→const PRINT_AREA_PADDING = 0.1\n    13→\n    14→/**\n    15→ * Get the print area rectangle for the canvas (with visual padding).\n    16→ * Since the canvas aspect ratio already matches the product, the print area\n    17→ * is the canvas minus padding.\n    18→ */\n    19→export function getCanvasPrintArea(\n    20→  _productCategory: string,\n    21→  canvasWidth: number,\n    22→  canvasHeight: number,\n    23→  _panelId: string = 'front'\n    24→): { left: number; top: number; width: number; height: number } {\n    25→  return {\n    26→    left: Math.round(canvasWidth * PRINT_AREA_PADDING),\n    27→    top: Math.round(canvasHeight * PRINT_AREA_PADDING),\n    28→    width: Math.round(canvasWidth * (1 - 2 * PRINT_AREA_PADDING)),\n    29→    height: Math.round(canvasHeight * (1 - 2 * PRINT_AREA_PADDING)),\n    30→  }\n    31→}\n    32→\n    33→/**\n    34→ * Get the product type key from a category string.\n    35→ */\n    36→export function getProductTypeFromCategory(category: string): string {\n    37→  const key = (category || '').toLowerCase().trim()\n    38→  return CATEGORY_TO_PRODUCT_TYPE[key] || 'tshirt'\n    39→}\n    40→\n    41→/**\n    42→ * Get the raw print area (unscaled, 1024×1024 reference) for a product type.\n    43→ * Kept for backward compatibility with composition-renderer and mockup-generator.\n    44→ */\n    45→export function getRawPrintArea(productType: string, panelId: string = 'front'): PrintArea {\n    46→  const multiPanels = MULTI_PANEL_AREAS[productType]\n    47→  if (multiPanels && multiPanels[panelId]) {\n    48→    return multiPanels[panelId]\n    49→  }\n    50→  return PRINT_AREAS[productType] || PRINT_AREAS['tshirt']\n    51→}\n    52→\n    53→/**\n    54→ * Get available panels for a product type.\n    55→ */\n    56→export function getAvailablePanels(productType: string): string[] {\n    57→  const multiPanels = MULTI_PANEL_AREAS[productType]\n    58→  if (multiPanels) {\n    59→    return Object.keys(multiPanels)\n    60→  }\n    61→  return ['front']\n    62→}\n    63→"}]},"uuid":"0ccd7f36-0328-4d9c-af93-1fc803ce4a9a","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:41:22.078Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4d26c5c1-13bb-45ff-9ad0-c5918e94022d"}

    1→/**\n     2→ * Fabric.js v6 initialization and font loading for Design Studio.\n     3→ * Uses dynamic import to avoid SSR issues.\n     4→ */\n     5→\n     6→const FONT_FILES: Record<string, string> = {\n     7→  'Inter': 'Inter-Regular.ttf',\n     8→  'Roboto': 'Roboto-Regular.ttf',\n     9→  'Montserrat': 'Montserrat-Regular.ttf',\n    10→  'Playfair Display': 'PlayfairDisplay-Regular.ttf',\n    11→  'Oswald': 'Oswald-Regular.ttf',\n    12→  'Lato': 'Lato-Regular.ttf',\n    13→  'Pacifico': 'Pacifico-Regular.ttf',\n    14→  'Dancing Script': 'DancingScript-Regular.ttf',\n    15→  'Great Vibes': 'GreatVibes-Regular.ttf',\n    16→  'Caveat': 'Caveat-Regular.ttf',\n    17→  'Permanent Marker': 'PermanentMarker-Regular.ttf',\n    18→  'Bebas Neue': 'BebasNeue-Regular.ttf',\n    19→}\n    20→\n    21→export const AVAILABLE_FONTS = Object.keys(FONT_FILES)\n    22→\n    23→let fontsLoaded = false\n    24→\n    25→export async function loadFonts(): Promise<void> {\n    26→  if (fontsLoaded) return\n    27→  const promises = Object.entries(FONT_FILES).map(([family, file]) => {\n    28→    const font = new FontFace(family, `url(/fonts/${file})`)\n    29→    return font.load().then(f => {\n    30→      document.fonts.add(f)\n    31→    }).catch(() => {\n    32→      // Font load failure is non-critical — fallback to system font\n    33→    })\n    34→  })\n    35→  await Promise.all(promises)\n    36→  fontsLoaded = true\n    37→}\n    38→\n    39→export async function loadFabric() {\n    40→  const fabric = await import('fabric')\n    41→  return fabric\n    42→}\n  39→  // Reds\n    40→  'red': '#dc143c',\n    41→  'burgundy': '#800020',\n    42→  'maroon': '#800000',\n    43→  'wine': '#722f37',\n    44→  'dark red': '#8b0000',\n    45→  'cardinal red':   88→  'tie-dye': '#ff69b4',\n    89→  'neon orange': '#ff6700',\n    90→  'neon yellow': '#ccff00',\n    91→  'neon pink': '#ff6ec7',\n    92→}\n    93→\n    94→/**\n    95→ * Convert a garment color name to hex code.\n    96→ * Falls back to a neutral gray if the color name is unknown.\n    97→ */\n    98→export function colorNameToHex(name: string): string {\n    99→  const normalized = name.toLowerCase().trim()\n   100→  return COLOR_NAME_TO_HEX[normalized] || '#808080'\n   101→}\n   102→\n   103→/**\n   104→ * Determine if a color is \"light\" (for text contrast purposes).\n   105→ * Uses relative luminance calculation.\n   106→ */\n   107→export function isLightColor(hex: string): boolean {\n   108→  const color = hex.replace('#', '')\n   109→  const r = parseInt(color.substring(0, 2), 16)\n   110→  const g = parseInt(color.substring(2, 4), 16)\n   111→  const b = parseInt(color.substring(4, 6), 16)\n   112→  // Relative luminance\n   113→  const 6→import { Switch } from '@/components/ui/switch'\n     7→import { ColorPicker } from './ColorPicker'\n     8→\n     9→interface ShadowControlProps {\n    10→  enabled: boolean\n    11→  color: string\n    12→  blur: number\n    13→  offsetX: number\n    14→  offsetY: number\n    15→  onToggle: (enabled: boolean) => void\n    16→  onColorChange: (color: string) => void\n    17→  onBlurChange: (blur: number) => void\n    18→  onOffsetXChange: (x: number) => void\n    19→  onOffsetYChange: (y: number) => void\n    20→}\n    21→\n    22→export function ShadowControl({\n    23→  enabled,\n    24→  color,\n    25→  blur,\n    26→  offsetX,\n    27→  offsetY,\n    28→  onToggle,\n    29→  onColorChange,\n    30→  onBlurChange,\n    31→  onOffsetXChange,\n    32→  onOffsetYChange,\n    33→}: ShadowControlProps) {\n    34→  const t = useTranslations('designEditor.effects')\n    35→\n    36→  return (\n    37→    <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n    38→      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n    39→        <Label className=\"text-xs\">{t('shadow')}</Label>\n    40→        <Switch checked={enabled} onCheckedChange={onToggle} />\n    41→      </div>\n    42→\n    43→      {enabled && (\n    44→        <div className=\"space-y-2 pl-1\">\n    45→          <ColorPicker value={color} onChange={onColorChange} />\n    46→\n    47→          <div className=\"space-y-1\">\n    48→            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n    49→              <span className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">{t('shadowBlur')}</span>\n    50→              <span className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground w-6 text-right\">{blur}</span>\n    51→            </div>\n    52→            <Slider value={[blur]} onValueChange={([v]) => onBlurChange(v)} min={0} max={30} step={1} />\n    53→          </div>\n    54→\n    55→          <div className=\"space-y-1\">\n    56→            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n    57→              <span className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">{t('shadowOffsetX')}</span>\n    58→              <span className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground w-6 text-right\">{offsetX}</span>\n    59→            </div>\n    60→            <Slider value={[offsetX]} onValueChange={([v]) => onOffsetXChange(v)} min={-20} max={20} step={1} />\n    61→          </div>\n    62→\n    63→          <div className=\"space-y-1\">\n    64→            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n    65→              <span className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">{t('shadowOffsetY')}</span>\n    66→              <span className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground w-6 text-right\">{offsetY}</span>\n    67→            </div>\n    68→            <Slider value={[offsetY]} onValueChange={([v]) => onOffsetYChange(v)} min={-20} max={20} step={1} />\n    69→          </div>\n    70→        </div>\n    71→      )}\n    72→    </div>\n    73→  )\n CanvasWorkspace.tsx:286 — Race condition on canvas.loadFromJSON()**\n- **File**: `/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend/src/components/design-studio/CanvasWorkspace.tsx`\n- **Line**: 286, 502\n- **Issue**: `canvas.loadFromJSON()` is async but not awaited in `useImperativeHandle.ref.current.loadFromJSON()`. This can cause the canvas to render before JSON is fully loaded, showing blank/partial designs.\n- **Fix**: Ensure the imperative `loadFromJSON` awaits properly:\n  ```typescript\n  loadFromJSON: async (json) => {\n    const canvas = fabricCanvasRef.current\n    if (!canvas) return\n    await canvas.loadFromJSON(json)\n    canvas.renderAll()\n  }\n  ```\n\n**2. CanvasWorkspace.tsx:193-220 — Blank image URL loading never completes gracefully**\n- **File**: `/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend/src/components/design-studio/CanvasWorkspace.tsx`\n- **Lines**: 193-220\n- **Issue**: If `blankImageUrl` (from Printful CDN) fails to load and no local template exists, the canvas falls back to `variantColorHex` but never validates it. If `variantColorHex` is undefined, `colorNameToHex()` gets called but may return a fallback gray, silently masking the missing image.\n- **Fix**: Add null check before setting backgroundColor:\n  ```typescript\n  if (!templateLoaded && !destroyed) {\n    const hex = variantColorHex || colorNameToHex(variantColor)\n    if (hex) canvas.backgroundColor = hex\n  }\n  ```\n\n**3. useCanvasHistory.ts:107 — Unhandled promise rejection in undo/redo**\n- **File**: `/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend/src/hooks/useCanvasHistory.ts`\n- **Lines**: 107, 132\n- **Issue**: `canvas.loadFromJSON()` promise is not caught. If loading fails, `isRestoring` remains true forever, blocking all future undo/redo.\n- **Fix**: Add error handler:\n  ```typescript\n  canvas.loadFromJSON(JSON.parse(json)).then(() => {\n    canvas.renderAll()\n    isRestoring.current = false\n    onAfterRestore?.()\n    notifyChange()\n  }).catch(() => {\n    isRestoring.current = false\n    console.error('Failed to restore undo state')\n  })\n  ```\n\n**4. CanvasWorkspace.tsx:357-382 — addText() not awaiting loadFabric()**\n- **File**: `/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend/src/components/design-studio/CanvasWorkspace.tsx`\n- **Lines**: 357-382\n- **Issue**: `loadFabric()` is a promise but not awaited. IText may be undefined if Fabric.js module hasn't loaded. Will throw \"Cannot read property 'IText' of undefined\" error.\n- **Fix**: Make addText async and await loadFabric:\n  ```typescript\n  addText: async (text, opts = {}) => {\n    const canvas = fabricCanvasRef.current\n    if (!canvas) return\n    const { IText } = await loadFabric()\n    const itext = new IText(text, { ... })\n    // ...rest of code\n  }\n  ```\n\n**5. CanvasWorkspace.tsx:484-490 — exportPNG() doesn't handle multiplier overflow**\n- **File**: `/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend/src/components/design-studio/CanvasWorkspace.tsx`\n- **Lines**: 483-490\n- **Issue**: iOS Safari has 256MB canvas memory limit. Calling `exportPNG(2)` on large compositions (e.g., 4800×3600) can cause out-of-memory crashes. No safeguard against excessive multipliers.\n- **Fix**: Cap multiplier and use capped DPR:\n  ```typescript\n  exportPNG: (multiplier = 2) => {\n    const canvas = fabricCanvasRef.current\n    if (!canvas) return ''\n    const capped = Math.min(multiplier, getCappedDPR())\n    return canvas.toDataURL({\n      format: 'png',\n      quality: 1,\n      multiplier: capped,\n    })\n  }\n  ```\n\n**6. DesignStudioPage.tsx:63-65 — useCanvasHistory not passing canvas ref correctly**\n- **File**: `/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend/src/components/design-studio/DesignStudioPage.tsx`\n- **Lines**: 62-65\n- **Issue**: `useCanvasHistory(fabricCanvasRef, ...)` receives `fabricCanvasRef` which is a ref to the **parent's** Fabric.Canvas instance. But `fabricCanvasRef` is updated in a 200ms polling loop (lines 100-107). The history hook may work with stale canvas refs before the interval updates it.\n- **Fix**: Pass the canvas properly via useEffect dependency or delay history initialization:\n  ```typescript\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!fabricCanvasRef.current) return\n    // Initialize history after canvas is ready\n    const timer = setTimeout(() => {\n      // saveState after canvas is stable\n    }, 300)\n    return () => clearTimeout(timer)\n  }, [fabricCanvasRef])\n  ```\n\n---\n\n### HIGH PRIORITY BUGS (Major UX issues)\n\n**7. But if a product has NO variants and is not in TEMPLATE_COLORS, the color selector is hidden (line 558) but `setVariantColor()` is never called, so no canvas background is rendered.\n- **Fix**: Ensure color is set even if no variants:\n  ```typescript\n  const availableColors = (variants?.colors && variants.colors.length > 0)\n    ? variants.colors\n    : (TEMPLATE_COLORS[product.productType] || ['white'])\n  \n  // Always set initial color\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (availableColors.length > 0 && !variantColor) {\n      setVariantColor(availableColors[0])\n    }\n  }, [availableColors, variantColor, setVariantColor])\n  ```\n\n**8.
